Borkeo EMR v48.0.0 - NPCA v1.4 guide alignment and NPCA approval pack
Base a5f92450088f86a03ed281898efffe832aeea040 (47.9.0)

Antigravity: read docs/development/NPCA_PDF_ALIGNMENT_V480_VALIDATION.md first.
Back up committed code (npm run backup:code), apply these complete UTF-8 payloads, preserve later unrelated customizations.
Never replace .env, hospital databases, uploads, backups or password exports.
NPCA stays turned off (NPCA_ENABLED=false) until NPCA gives the test credentials.
Run npm test and npm run check:khmer before restarting only the verified project launcher.
Verified: npm test 1060/1060 passed; npm run check:khmer passed (project folder and disposable copy).

Install, one step at a time:
1. Close the running EMR window (Start-Borkeo-WiFi).
2. This project folder already contains v48.0 (committed and pushed to GitHub master).
   On another computer with a Git copy run: git pull
   Without Git: give this file to Antigravity and ask it to apply the payloads below.
3. Start Start-Borkeo-WiFi.bat again.
4. Open the NPCA page and press Ctrl+F5 once so the browser loads the new page.
5. For NPCA: send docs/npca/NPCA-V1.4-CONFORMANCE.md, docs/npca/OPEN-QUESTIONS.md
   and the folder docs/npca/approval-pack/.

# Task context for Codex, Gemini or OpenCode

## NPCA v1.4 PDF alignment and approval pack - v48.0.0, 26 October 2026

User asked to integrate the EMR with the NPCA API following the v1.4 PDF guide
and to do as much as possible towards approval; after the gap review the user
said "yes code for all". Clean master a5f9245 (47.9.0); branch
feature/npca-v14-pdf-alignment. Pre-update code snapshot:
C:\Users\Local-admin\Borkeo_EMR_Code_Backups\v47.9.0-a5f92450-1a4c4d24

The v42.8 integration already covered the PDF flow. v48.0 adds: current
Registration positions for marital status / occupation / disability (the old
positions blocked every patient), occupation + disabilities, encounter class,
PDF field names next to the workbook names, single-CMS-code check, the 4.4
date-range status check (POST /api/npca/check-processed, NPCA page section),
and the approval pack (scripts/build-npca-approval-pack.mjs,
docs/npca/approval-pack/, docs/npca/NPCA-V1.4-CONFORMANCE.md, questions 22-27).
Still external: NPCA credentials, test-server acceptance, service codes.
See NPCA_PDF_ALIGNMENT_V480_VALIDATION.md.

## Hosting test patients - v47.9.0, 26 October 2026

User approved ("yes code it") 30 complete synthetic patients for the GitHub
Codespace test hosting. Clean master b839489 (47.8.0); branch
feature/registration-hosting-test-patients. Pre-update code snapshot:
C:\Users\Local-admin\Borkeo_EMR_Code_Backups\v47.8.0-b8394899-5e379cfb

scripts/seed-hosting-test-patients.mjs builds on the v46.5 complete
registration fixture (IDs 160201-YYYYMMDD-###, same photos), adds street
address, entry types, HEF / NSSF / other-card numbers (never fake
verification), triage measurements (TRIAGE_WAITING, no service), and makes the
1234 accounts usable. It refuses a database with any non-synthetic patient.
Codespace steps: docs/DEPLOY.md. The previously proposed browser autosave draft
was put aside by the user ("forget this") and is not implemented.
See HOSTING_TEST_PATIENTS_V479_VALIDATION.md.

## ANC orders sent once per visit - v47.8.0, 26 October 2026

User approved ("yes", scope once per visit) a notice after every ANC order /
doctor request and sending each only once. Clean master d18bc5c (47.7.0);
branch feature/maternity-anc-orders-once. Pre-update code snapshot:
C:\Users\Local-admin\Borkeo_EMR_Code_Backups\v47.7.0-d18bc5c8-1f411f3c

Server: ancSentOrders / ancOrderAlreadySent (server/maternal.mjs) - sent today
for any of the woman's encounters, or an unreleased earlier order; used by the
midwife branch of POST /api/paraclinic/orders and by POST
/api/maternal/ask-doctor (409 code ALREADY_SENT) and returned as
`sentOrders` by GET /api/anc/record. Page: orderButton shows the note,
sendOnce + showToast, run() keeps a returned message. Pending: Khmer for
anc.labsHint (shows English). See ANC_ORDERS_V478_VALIDATION.md.

normalized service, validates that number, and uses it for the existing skip
operation. It never allocates a new number or rewrites registration/clinical

patient, an inactive call or another service. Existing role/ward/CSRF guards,

# NPCA v1.4 PDF alignment and approval pack v48.0.0

Approved update (owner said "yes code for all" on 26 October 2026, after the
comparison of the EMR with the NPCA "EMR Clinical Data Integration
Specification v1.4" PDF). Base master a5f9245 (47.9.0); branch
feature/npca-v14-pdf-alignment. NPCA stays turned off by default.

## What changed

1. Registration data for NPCA
   - Marital status, occupation and disability are read from the positions
     the current Registration form saves (marital 32-35, occupation 37,
     disability 38/39; server/npca/registration-fields.mjs).
   - Before, the marital status was read from old positions (22-25), so every
     current patient was blocked with "marital status unmapped".
   - "Other" occupation sends the typed occupation.
   - `occupation` and `disabilities` are sent only when there is a value.
2. Encounter `class` ("OPD", "IPD", "EMERGENCY") is sent, as in PDF 5.1.
3. PDF names are sent next to the workbook names:
   - `diagnosis_type` next to `diagnosis_type_name`;
   - `medicine_code` next to `code`, and `form`, in prescription medicines.
4. Only single CMS codes (2 letters and 3-4 digits) pass the local check. 9
   catalogue rows have a note or two codes in the code column (OPEN-QUESTIONS
   27); the catalogue itself is unchanged.
5. Date-range status check (PDF 4.4):
   - POST /api/npca/check-processed, for cashier, accountant and ADMIN, with
     CSRF.
   - The NPCA page has a "check status by date" section (Khmer by default, 7
     approved strings).
   - Pages are followed, and statuses are read as numbers or as their names.
6. Approval pack for NPCA:
   - scripts/build-npca-approval-pack.mjs;
   - docs/npca/approval-pack/, with the OPD, IPD, transfer, referral and ANC
     form samples, all built from synthetic visits and passing the validation;
   - docs/npca/NPCA-V1.4-CONFORMANCE.md, mapping every PDF section;
   - OPEN-QUESTIONS 22-27.

No clinical, pharmacy, laboratory or financial logic and no patient data were
changed. The 3-digit / note codes in the medicine catalogue were not edited.

===== BEGIN FILE: docs/npca/INTEGRATION.md =====
# Borkeo EMR NPCA integration — v42.8.0

## v48.0.0 changes (NPCA v1.4 PDF comparison)

- Marital status, occupation and disability are read from the current Registration
  positions (server/npca/registration-fields.mjs, checked in the browser by
  tests/npca-registration-fields-browser.mjs). Before v48.0 the marital status was
  read from old positions, so every real patient was blocked as "unmapped".
- `occupation` and `disabilities` are sent when present; encounters carry `class`;
  diagnoses carry `diagnosis_type` and `diagnosis_type_name`; prescription
  medicines carry `code`, `medicine_code` and `form`.
- Only single CMS codes pass the local check (OPEN-QUESTIONS.md 27).
- The NPCA page has a date-range status check (cashier, accountant, ADMIN).
- Approval pack for NPCA: docs/npca/approval-pack/ and NPCA-V1.4-CONFORMANCE.md.

NPCA is disabled by default. No real patient data was sent during development.
The integration was tested with synthetic patients and a loopback HTTP mock.
Passing those tests is not NPCA certification.

## What is ready

- ADMIN connection settings and token test at `/admin/npca`. Director can read settings but cannot test, map, submit, or resubmit.
- Registration's additional NPCA beneficiary search. Existing HEF and NSSF lookups remain available. Select the household member, check identity, then save registration. The server validates the search selection and saves SPID, NPCA patient code, cards, and payment scheme additively. An expired, ineligible, mismatched, or ambiguous selection cannot become a claim. Identity/card edits invalidate the previous claim link until verification is repeated.
- A local reference snapshot extracted from all three original v1.4 Excel workbooks. `server/npca/standards.json` records source SHA-256 hashes. `scripts/import-npca-standards.py` rebuilds it using the Python standard library; Python is not needed to run the EMR.
- A visit builder, local validation, durable SQLite queue, retries, reconciliation, polling, correction links, payload review, and record/Billing status badges.
- Supplemental clinical fields for maternal and child forms use the official Excel labels and values. They are optional for normal EMR work and required only when the NPCA validation rules require them. The existing clinical forms and their saved fields are preserved.
- A synthetic mock and tests in `tests/helpers/npca-mock.mjs` and `tests/v428-npca.test.mjs`.

## What is sent

A single `{"visits":[...]}` payload contains the patient identity, verified SPID,
NCDD-coded address, visit, encounters, one initial triage, vital signs, saved
clinical histories/examinations, released lab/imaging results, ICD-10 diagnoses,
SOAP notes, prescriptions with CMS and explicit dispense, invoices, referrals,
and applicable structured forms. Unreleased results are excluded. Patient photos,
signature images, local file paths, and private attachment URLs are not exported.
Imaging reports use text unless a reachable image URL is explicitly available.

Patient code remains the Borkeo Health ID, falling back to the patient key. The
NPCA-assigned patient code is retained locally for reconciliation; ask NPCA to
confirm that relationship. Visit and encounter codes are deterministic and stay
the same through correction and OPD-to-IPD transfer. Times use `+07:00`.

Payloads are stored in `npca_submissions` for authorized local review. They are
not written into the audit log. Audit entries record actions, actor, visit, and
status, without passwords, tokens, card numbers, or full payloads. Tokens remain
in server memory. Credentials are read from `.env` and are never displayed.

## When a visit may be submitted

1. Verify the beneficiary in Registration and save the correct member.
2. Finish the clinical work. OPD requires completion/discharge (normally the
   pharmacy receipt); IPD requires discharge. A doctor-signed ANC referral is a
   completed referral. HEF Poor IPD may first be submitted after admission and
   then updated after discharge with the same visit code.
3. A Cashier (`CLERK`), Accountant, or ADMIN opens `/npca`, reviews the payload,
   fixes every listed problem, and selects Submit. Clinical staff completing a
   note do not independently authorize an external claim.
4. The background worker processes authorized queued visits one at a time. It
   scans every 30 seconds. The queue survives a server restart or internet loss.
5. Status 5 is Processing, 3 Pending, 2 Failed, and 1 Ready. Status 5/3 records
   are polled every eight hours by default. Ready/Failed records stop polling.
6. Correct Failed records, or finish Pending admissions, and submit again.
   The payload must change; its visit code does not. Ready and Processing
   records cannot be resubmitted from the local interface.

After an ambiguous timeout or HTTP 500, the worker checks the processed endpoint
before retrying the stable visit code. Network retries use exponential backoff,
capped at eight hours. HTTP 400/401-after-refresh/403/422 stop for correction.

## Credentials and configuration — one step at a time

1. Ask the NPCA integration team for a test environment, OAuth password-grant
   client ID and secret, service username/password, system code, and official
   health-facility code. Ask them to answer `OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`.
2. Back up the hospital database through the existing EMR backup procedure.
3. Copy `.env.example` to `.env` only if `.env` does not already exist. Otherwise
   add the NPCA settings to the existing file. Never replace unrelated settings.
4. Leave `NPCA_ENABLED=false` while entering the credentials. Keep `.env` local;
   never add it to GitHub or the update text file.
5. Set `NPCA_BASE_URL` to the HTTPS origin NPCA supplies. There is no default
   external destination. Do not use the example domains from Postman as a
   production address without NPCA's approval.
6. Fill `NPCA_CLIENT_ID`, `NPCA_CLIENT_SECRET`, `NPCA_USERNAME`, `NPCA_PASSWORD`,
   `NPCA_SYSTEM_CODE`, and `NPCA_FACILITY_CODE` with NPCA's values.
7. Set the endpoint path/method pairs to the values NPCA confirms. Defaults are
   from the supplied Postman collection: token POST `/oauth/token`, search GET
   `/api/v1/external/beneficiary/search`, submit POST `/api/v1/external/visits`,
   processed GET `/api/v1/external/visits/processed`.
8. Keep `NPCA_POLL_HOURS=8` and `NPCA_TIMEOUT_MS=15000` unless NPCA recommends a
   change. `NPCA_SEND_FORMS=true` is the default; set false if NPCA confirms its
   production v1.3 endpoint cannot yet accept the v1.4 forms.
9. Only after NPCA confirms the test environment and authorizes synthetic test
   submissions, set `NPCA_ENABLED=true` and restart the EMR.
10. Log in as ADMIN, open `/admin/npca`, and use Test connection. It shows the
    last successful token time and never shows credentials or tokens.
11. Enter only NPCA-confirmed service-code mappings. Other code overrides must
    target a value in the official workbook. A missing mapping blocks the claim.
12. Run synthetic OPD, IPD, transfer, referral, ANC/PNC, delivery, contraception,
    abortion, and child cases in a separate test database. Obtain NPCA's
    acceptance of the actual payloads before changing to production credentials.
13. Enable production only after NPCA/hospital approval. No hospital database,
    uploads, or backups need to be moved to another host for this integration.

To stop outbound integration, set `NPCA_ENABLED=false` and restart. Local EMR
work continues, and queued/submitted records remain in the local database.

## Correcting validation problems

Open Review on the NPCA page. Each local problem names the field and links to
Registration, Triage, the clinical record, ANC, or Billing. Fill missing data on
that screen, save, and review again. The review dialog also contains a collapsed
technical payload view. It does not send anything.

- A free-text medicine without a CMS code, an unknown ICD-10 code, or a missing
  dispense quantity blocks submission. No code or clinical normal value is guessed.
- Required clinician/provider metadata must be present. New/changed daily notes
  get a small server-authenticated authorship snapshot; unchanged older notes
  are not reassigned to today's doctor. Older notes without authorship may need
  clinical review and correction before export.
- The last saved triage must precede consultation/admission. If a later edit
  replaced the historical triage and the earlier measurements are unavailable,
  the integration blocks instead of inventing the original observations.
- NPCA service codes, mixed-currency rules, and invoice-level discount allocation
  require confirmation. Unconfirmed mappings/allocations block submission; existing
  billing calculations are not changed.
- Referral facility code, reference number, transport, doctor phone, and public
  document URL may need the additional referral fields on the clinical record.
  The integration never publishes a private attachment to create a URL.
- Form fields not stored structurally in older forms can be entered in the
  additional NPCA assessment panel. Unknown value sets, including the missing
  Positivity list, still block until NPCA resolves them.

## Local verification

Run tests in a disposable project copy, without `.env`, patient databases,
backups, or patient uploads. Use synthetic fixtures only. The mock's insecure
HTTP exception is programmatic and limited to 127.0.0.1/::1; no `.env` switch
allows insecure remote HTTP.

Run `npm test`, then `npm run check:khmer`. The suite uses four test workers
because older regression tests share a synthetic demo fixture. New NPCA tests
use in-memory SQLite and the local mock.

## Remaining external acceptance work

Credentials, endpoint/encoding confirmation, actual beneficiary eligibility
shape, service mappings, missing value sets, referral document policy, invoice
currency/discount rules, and NPCA's test-server approval remain external work.
No live NPCA connection, production certification, external upload, or browser
acceptance test against NPCA was performed. See `OPEN-QUESTIONS.md` for the
precise source inconsistencies and choices.
===== END FILE: docs/npca/INTEGRATION.md =====

===== BEGIN FILE: docs/npca/NPCA-V1.4-CONFORMANCE.md =====
# Borkeo EMR: conformance with the NPCA EMR Clinical Data Integration Specification v1.4

For the NPCA integration team (Department of Data Management and Information
Technology). Borkeo EMR version 48.0.0. This document maps every section of the
specification to the EMR implementation, so NPCA can review it before
connecting the EMR to the test environment.

Status of the work: the integration is built and tested locally with synthetic
patients and a local test server that copies the NPCA API. **It has not yet been
connected to the NPCA test environment**: credentials, the system code and the
health facility code are still needed (section 3). NPCA is turned off by
default and nothing is sent until an administrator turns it on.

The sample payloads requested in "Related documents" are in
[`approval-pack/`](approval-pack/README.md).

## Summary

| Spec | Topic | Status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1.4 | REST JSON over HTTPS, ICD-10, ISO-8601 `+07:00`, NCDD address | Done |
| 2.1 / 4.1 | OAuth 2.0 password grant, Bearer token, refresh | Done |
| 2.1 / 4.2 | Beneficiary search before submission, SPID | Done |
| 2.1 / 4.3 | Visit submission, `{"visits":[...]}` | Done |
| 2.1 / 4.4 / 4.5 | Processing result, polling every 8 hours, statuses 1/2/3/5 | Done |
| 4.4 | Date-range check (`discharged_at_from/to`, `admitted_at_from/to`) | Done in v48.0 |
| 2.2.1 / 2.2.2 | Incomplete case (status 3) and complete case (5 → 1 or 2) | Done |
| 4.3.2 | Local validation before sending, duplicate visit check | Done |
| 4.5.3 / 4.5.5 / 4.5.6 | Status and failure reason shown; correct and resubmit | Done |
| 4.6 | Success, 400, 401, 403, 422, 500 handling | Done |
| 5 / 6 / 7 | All base, core and encounter components | Done (see notes) |
| 8 | Clinical data forms, the 7 form codes | Done |
| 3 | NPCA credentials, system code, facility code | **Waiting for NPCA** |

## 1. Overview and standards (spec 1.4)

- Every call is HTTPS. The EMR refuses an address that is not HTTPS, has a
  user name or password in it, or has a path. Plain HTTP is accepted only by
  the automated tests, on the computer itself (127.0.0.1).
- Diagnoses use ICD-10 codes from the EMR ICD-10 list. A code not in the list
  blocks the submission; no code is guessed.
- All dates and times are sent as `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss.sss+07:00` in Cambodia
  time, whatever the time zone of the computer. Birth dates are `YYYY-MM-DD`.
- Addresses use the NCDD codes and Khmer names of province, district, commune
  and village from the gazetteer in the EMR. An address that is not an exact
  NCDD match blocks the submission.
- Sex, marital status, nationality, admission type, visit type, discharge type,
  visit outcome, payment type, vital-sign names, medical-history sections,
  physical-examination systems and form values are sent only as codes or names
  from the NPCA v1.4 reference workbook (`external_standard_data_references_v1.4.xlsx`).
  A value outside the workbook blocks the submission.

## 2. Integration flow (spec 2)

| Spec step | In the EMR |
| --- | --- |
| Endpoint 1 Authentication | The token is requested once and kept in server memory until shortly before `expires_in`. It is refreshed with the refresh token, and after a 401 a new token is requested once before the call is repeated. Tokens and passwords are never written to disk, to the audit log or to the screen. |
| Endpoint 2 Beneficiary search | Registration: the clerk enters the card number, the EMR shows the household members found, the clerk chooses the patient. The SPID, NPCA patient code and benefit cards are saved with the patient. A dead patient, a patient without explicit eligibility or without SPID cannot be used for a claim. A later check that no longer finds the patient eligible removes the claim link. |
| Endpoint 3 Visit submission | Cashier, accountant or ADMIN opens the NPCA page, reviews the payload and the list of problems, and submits. A queue in the database sends the visits one at a time, keeps them through a power cut or internet loss, and tries again later. |
| Endpoint 4 Tracking | After status 5 or 3 the EMR asks for the result every 8 hours (configurable). It stops when the status is 1 Ready or 2 Failed. |
| Step 3.1 Submission initiation | OPD: after the treatment is finished (pharmacy dispensing or OPD discharge). IPD: after discharge. IPD HEF Poor: may be sent after admission (status 3) and updated after discharge with the same visit code. |
| Step 3.2 EMR portal validation | Required fields, data format, reference codes and the linking rules of 4.3.2 are checked before anything is sent; each problem is shown with a link to the screen where it is corrected. |

## 3. Security and authentication (spec 3)

| Header | Value |
| --- | --- |
| `Authorization` | `Bearer <access_token>` |
| `Content-Type` / `Accept` | `application/json` |
| `X-System-Code` | from the `NPCA_SYSTEM_CODE` setting, given by NPCA |
| `X-Health-Facility-Code` | from the `NPCA_FACILITY_CODE` setting, given by NPCA |

The client ID, client secret, user name and password are kept in the server
`.env` file of the hospital and are never shown, logged or sent anywhere other
than the NPCA token endpoint. Only ADMIN can test the connection. Redirects are
refused, every call has a time limit (15 s by default).

**Needed from NPCA:** test environment address, client ID and secret, user name
and password, system code, health facility code.

## 4. API endpoints (spec 4)

| Spec | Method and path used | Setting |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 4.1 | `POST /oauth/token` | `NPCA_TOKEN_PATH` |
| 4.2 | `GET /api/v1/external/beneficiary/search?card_number=` | `NPCA_SEARCH_PATH` |
| 4.3 | `POST /api/v1/external/visits` | `NPCA_SUBMIT_PATH` |
| 4.4 | `GET /api/v1/external/visits/processed` | `NPCA_PROCESSED_PATH` |

Section 4.3 writes the submission path as `/api/external/v1/visits` while 4.2
and 4.4 use `/api/v1/external/...`; the EMR uses `/api/v1/external/visits` (as in
the NPCA Postman collection) and the path can be changed in the settings
without a code change. **Please confirm the path.**

- **4.3 response:** `status`, `visit_ulid` (also `uuld` as written in 4.3) and
  `visit_code` are saved.
- **4.3.2 duplicate visit check:** every visit has one stable visit code. A visit
  that is Ready (1) or Processing (5), or already in the queue, cannot be sent
  again. A Failed (2) or Pending (3) visit is sent again with the same visit
  code, and only after its data has changed.
- **4.4 processing endpoint:**
  - Each submitted visit is checked with `visit_code`.
  - Since v48.0 the NPCA page also has a date-range check. It sends
    `discharged_at_from` / `discharged_at_to` (or `admitted_at_from` /
    `admitted_at_to`) with `per_page=100`, follows the pages (`last_page`), and
    updates every visit this EMR submitted.
  - The status is read as a number (5, 3, 2, 1) or as the name in 4.5.4
    (processing, pending, failed, ready).
- **4.5.3 to 4.5.6:**
  - The NPCA page shows Processing / Pending / Failed / Ready (in Khmer by
    default) and the failure reason returned by NPCA.
  - The visit has a Review button and a link to the screen to correct.
  - After correction it is submitted again.
- **4.6:** the HTTP status decides what happens; `success:false` is never taken
  as success.
  - 400, 403 and 422 stop the visit and show the reason for correction.
  - After a 401 the token is renewed once.
  - A 500 or no answer can mean NPCA received the visit, so the EMR first asks
    the processing endpoint and only then sends the same visit code again, with
    waiting times that grow up to 8 hours.

## 5–7. Components

| Spec | Component | Source in the EMR | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 5.1 | Encounter | OPD consultation, IPD admission, emergency, surgery, daily progress note | Stable codes; `class` "OPD" / "IPD" / "EMERGENCY" is sent (v48.0); surgery takes the visit class, progress notes IPD. |
| 5.2 | Observations | vital signs, history, examination | `value_type` and `value_unit` as in 5.2.2. |
| 6.1 | Patient | Registration | Health ID as `code`, SPID from the beneficiary search, NCDD address, identifications from the benefit cards. `occupation` and `disabilities` are sent when Registration has them (v48.0). Photos are not sent (no public URL). |
| 6.2 | Visit | Registration, triage, discharge | `health_facility_code` from the settings; admission, discharge, outcome and visit type as workbook codes; `followup_at` when an appointment exists. |
| 6.3 | Triage | Triage screen | Exactly one triage, recorded before the first consultation, with height and weight. |
| 6.4 | Vital signs | Triage and IPD daily monitoring | All six required signs at triage; random glucose when measured. Linked to the triage or progress note code. |
| 6.5 | Medical histories | IPD / OPD history fields and the allergy list | The nine section names of the workbook; empty sections are not sent (6.5.4 note). |
| 6.6 | Physical examinations | IPD examination | The eleven system names of the workbook. |
| 6.7 | Laboratories | Laboratory results released by the laboratory | Results not yet released are never sent. Reference range, interpretation, verified and recorded by / at. |
| 6.8 | Imageries | Imaging reports released by imaging | Report text and conclusion. Images are sent only when they have a public HTTPS address; patient images are never made public for this. |
| 6.9 | Diagnosis | ICD-10 admission and discharge diagnoses | `In` at admission, `Out` at discharge. Sent as `diagnosis_type` (PDF) and `diagnosis_type_name` (workbook) since v48.0. |
| 6.10 | SOAPs | OPD note, IPD daily notes | Linked to the OPD encounter or the progress note. |
| 6.11 | Prescriptions | OPD, IPD admission, daily notes, discharge medicines | CMS code as `code` (workbook) and `medicine_code` (PDF), `form` (v48.0); morning / afternoon / evening / night or `interval`; `days`; dispensed quantity and unit. Home medicines have `encounter_code` null (6.11.2 note). A medicine without a CMS code, or without dispensed quantity, blocks the submission. |
| 6.12 | Referral | IPD discharge "referred", ANC doctor-signed referral | Receiving facility name and code, reference number, transport, reason, caretaker, doctor and phone, medicines. |
| 6.13 | Consultations | Advice given to the patient | Array of strings. |
| 6.14 | Invoices | Billing | Payment type from the verified benefit card; services with NPCA service codes confirmed by the administrator; medicines with CMS codes. |
| 7.1–7.5 | Inpatient, outpatient, emergency, surgery, progress notes | As above | Surgery from the operative note and anaesthesia record. |

## 8. Clinical data forms

The seven forms of 8.1 are supported, with their fields and value sets taken
from `clinical_data_form_v1.4.xlsx`:

| Code | Form | Filled from |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ENTY-000H014 | OPD ANC | ANC record: gravida, gestational age, fundal height, fetal heart rate, LMP |
| ENTY-000001B2 | OPD PNC | PNC service and the NPCA assessment panel |
| ENTY-000016Z | Birth control | Family planning service and the NPCA assessment panel |
| ENTY-0000K1B | Abortion | Safe abortion service and the NPCA assessment panel |
| ENTY-0000K15 | Delivery | Obstetric ward, newborn record and the NPCA assessment panel |
| ENTY-00000A8C | Child examination 0–28 days | Newborn / paediatric (age ≤ 28 days) |
| ENTY-00000A9M | Child examination 1 month–5 years | Paediatric (age ≤ 5 years); see question 8 in OPEN-QUESTIONS.md, the PDF writes ENTY-000001E9 |

Each form has `visit_code`, `encounter_code` (the OPD or IPD encounter),
`form_code`, `form_name` and `form_data`, as in 8.2.

## Privacy

- No patient photo, signature image or private file address is sent.
- The payloads are kept in the hospital database so authorised staff can review
  them; the audit log records who did what, without card numbers, payloads,
  tokens or passwords.
- Only cashier, accountant and ADMIN can submit; the director can only read.
  Doctors and nurses cannot submit claims.

## What NPCA is asked to do

1. Provide the test environment and the credentials of section 3.
2. Review the sample payloads in `approval-pack/` and the questions in
   [`OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`](OPEN-QUESTIONS.md) (in particular 1, 4, 5, 6, 8, 11 and
   22 to 27).
3. Provide the NPCA service codes for the hospital's services (6.14.2), and the
   receiving facility codes for referrals.
4. Accept test submissions of synthetic OPD, IPD, transfer, referral and form
   cases on the test server before production credentials are issued.

===== BEGIN FILE: docs/npca/OPEN-QUESTIONS.md =====
# NPCA v1.4 questions requiring NPCA confirmation

Do not enable production submission until NPCA approves the test payloads.

1. Endpoints: this supplied Postman collection actually uses GET `/api/v1/external/beneficiary/search`, POST `/api/v1/external/visits`, GET `/api/v1/external/visits/processed`, and POST `/oauth/token`. The PDF submission path is `/api/external/v1/visits`; another documented beneficiary path is POST `/api/v1/beneficiaries/search`. All paths and methods are settings. No automatic fallback sends a payload to a different endpoint.
2. OAuth: Postman sends URL-encoded token grants; the update prompt specifies JSON headers on every call. This implementation sends JSON. Confirm the server accepts JSON password and refresh grants. The token request has no Bearer token until authentication succeeds.
3. `sample-json/visit_sample_data_form.json` is malformed near line 294. It is not parsed as an integration template.
4. Excel `API Objects` is authoritative: use flattened visit fields with `patient` and the plural component arrays, `request_code`, `diagnoses`, singular `referral`, trimmed `title`, `soaps`, `progress_notes`; no `class`/`class_name` field is added because neither is defined for the concrete Excel encounter models. Confirm these choices against the server validator.
5. Prescription medication identifier is `code` in the data-structure workbook, not `medicine_code`; invoice medications use `medicine_code`. CMS is emitted as `code` in prescriptions. The workbook has no separate medication `form` field. Confirm whether NPCA wants that extra field. Form/strength remain available in local review and the medicine catalogue.
6. Diagnosis uses `diagnosis_type_name` with `In`, `Out`, `Primary`, etc. The Standard Lookup also provides DIAGT codes. This field is explicitly a name, so names from the Diagnosis Types sheet are sent. `diagnosis_name` is incorrectly typed datetime in Excel; it is handled as string. Confirm both corrections.
7. Referral health-facility `name` and `code` descriptions are interchanged in one table. Send the name as name, the official code as code; require both. Do not invent CPA3 facility codes or public referral document URLs. No clinical attachment is exposed publicly just to satisfy this field.
8. Child 1 month–5 years uses `ENTY-00000A9M` from Excel, which differs from the PDF. Excel sheet title says 1Y–5Y, while the form itself says 1M–5Y. Forms are gated by `NPCA_SEND_FORMS` (default true).
9. Beneficiary response does not fully specify eligibility flags, card validity periods, or household response wrappers. The initial adapter accepts `data` arrays or `data.beneficiaries`/`beneficiaries`, explicit `dead:false`, explicit `eligible:true` or `eligibility:true/eligible`, and a SPID. Missing eligibility blocks claims. Confirm the live response before enabling. Multiple distinct benefit schemes block automatic selection; NPCA must confirm how the clerk chooses the applicable scheme.
10. Payment mapping: HEF_POOR/HEF_EQUITY → PATY-0000N09; HEF_VULNERABLE/HEF_VULNERABLE_FAMILY → PATY-00003LD; NSSF_WORKER → PATY-0000G3E; NSSF_CIVIL → PATY-00B003F; generic NSSF → PATY-000K010; CASH → PATY-00T001A; DISCOUNT → PATY-0000H08; EXEMPT/FULL_WAIVER → PATY-0J0000C. Generic INSURANCE, NGO, PAID, NSSF_OUTSIDE, and unspecified DIRECT are not inferred. They require an administrator-reviewed mapping to an existing official code. Patient benefit cards determine the verified claim scheme, not a guessed default.
11. No NPCA service-code catalogue is included in these workbooks. Borkeo service codes (OPD_CONSULT, ward charges, lab and imaging charges, etc.) are not automatically NPCA service codes. Administrators must record NPCA-confirmed service mappings; unmapped billed services block submission. Currency/rate and discount semantics must also be confirmed, particularly mixed-currency invoices.
12. Nationality/ethnicity: use only the provided Nationalities codes, including KHM. Borkeo values outside that list block. Free-text route/method and encounter ward/service names are retained because those fields have no code list in Excel.
13. The Medical Histories list has nine sections, and physical examination has eleven. The clinical form includes `Abdomen` and `Past Surgery History`, whereas reference names are `Gastro-Intestinal System` and `Past Surgical History`. Do not silently invent additional sections. Official spelling `Central Nervouse System` is preserved.
14. The Form Structure refers to `Birht Control Type` (typo), `Positivity`, and `Presentations`; the supplied value sets need careful confirmation before converting any free-text or boolean field. Unknown values block, rather than defaulting to normal/negative. No required clinical measurement, provider, diagnosis, or dispense quantity is invented.
15. Resubmission: the PDF describes restrictions but does not enumerate all lock/duplicate rules. Locally refuse Ready and Processing resubmission; allow corrections to Failed and updates to Pending only with a changed payload, retaining the same visit code. Reconcile ambiguous timeouts through the processed endpoint before retrying. Confirm external locking behavior and idempotency.
16. Processing query: PDF says `status`; Postman says `processing_status`. Single-visit polling uses `visit_code`, avoiding that conflict. Confirm whether a code-filtered request returns one object or a paginated list. Accept `visit_ulid` and the PDF's `uuld` typo only as response identifiers.
17. The PDF's HTTP-500 example contains body `http_code:403`. HTTP transport status controls retry; a business `success:false` response is never accepted as success.
18. Required fields not stored structurally by a given Borkeo form remain validation issues with correction links. A locally constructed payload is not evidence of NPCA certification. NPCA's test server must accept the actual OPD/IPD/transfer/referral/maternal/child examples before production.
19. Form Structure value-set labels differ from Form Values: `Birht Control Type` → `Birth Control Type`; `Presentations` → `Presentation`; `Delivery Types` → `Delivery Type`; `Contraceptive Method Type` → `Type Of Contraceptive Method Received`; singular Newborn/Child Examination Result → plural Results. These are name aliases only; OBVA codes still come from the workbook. `Positivity` is absent altogether and cannot be invented.
20. Patient `code` uses the stable Borkeo Health ID (patient key as fallback), as requested. The beneficiary's `patient_code` is stored locally but is not substituted for that identity without NPCA confirmation.
21. New daily-note authorship is captured additively on changed notes only. Older notes may lack reliable historical provider metadata. Latest saved triage can also be later than the initial consultation after an edit. Such cases block rather than assigning today's provider or inventing earlier measurements.

## Added in v48.0.0 (comparison with the v1.4 PDF)

22. Field names that differ between the PDF and the data-structure workbook are now sent under both names until NPCA confirms one: `diagnosis_type` (PDF 6.9.2) next to `diagnosis_type_name` (workbook), and `medicine_code` (PDF 6.11.3) next to `code` (workbook) in prescription medicines, which also carry `form` as in the PDF example. Confirm the name NPCA validates, and that the extra field is accepted.
23. The PDF base Encounter (5.1) requires `class` ("IPD", "OPD", "EMERGENCY"); the workbook encounter models do not define it, and the 5.1 example writes `class_name`. `class` is now sent on outpatients, inpatients and emergencies; surgeries take the class of the visit and progress notes "IPD". Confirm the name and the value for surgeries and progress notes.
24. The workbook requires `referral.referred_to.document_file` as a public HTTPS address of the referral letter; the PDF (6.12) does not list it. The EMR never makes a patient document public to fill it. Confirm whether it is required and how the letter should be provided.
25. `disabilities` (6.1.1, array of strings): Registration records only disabled / not disabled. A disabled patient is sent as one entry, the Khmer word for "disabled"; nothing is sent otherwise. Confirm whether NPCA has a list of disability values.
26. Processing endpoint date range (4.4): the EMR sends `discharged_at_from` / `discharged_at_to` (or the admitted pair) with `per_page=100` and `page`, and follows `last_page` from `data.last_page`, `meta.last_page` or `last_page` (at most 50 pages). Status may be a number or the 4.5.4 name. Confirm the paging parameters and the response shape.
27. CMS medicine codes: only single codes of two letters and 3 to 4 digits are accepted locally. Nine rows of the medicine catalogue have a note (`Core`, `Comp`, `WHO`, `Reserve 9th`, `A.lt of Clarif`) or two codes (`AA0410, AA0411` for Paracetamol 500 mg tablet) in the code column; a prescription of these medicines is blocked until the hospital enters the correct single CMS code. Confirm that NC009 / NJ060-style codes (3 digits) are valid CMS codes.
===== END FILE: docs/npca/OPEN-QUESTIONS.md =====

===== BEGIN FILE: docs/npca/approval-pack/README.md =====
# NPCA approval pack: sample JSON

For the NPCA integration team. These are the samples listed under "Related
documents" in the EMR Clinical Data Integration Specification v1.4. Each file
is the exact request body Borkeo EMR 48.0.0 would POST to the visit submission
endpoint. They were made by the EMR's own payload builder from **synthetic
visits** (no patient data) and passed the same validation that runs before
every real submission, using the real ICD-10 list, CMS medicine codes and NCDD
village list. The results are in [VALIDATION.md](VALIDATION.md).

| File | Related document | What it shows |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [opd-sample.json](opd-sample.json) | OPD Sample JSON | OPD consultation, triage and vital signs, history, examination, laboratory result, SOAP, home prescription with dispense, invoice, consultations, follow-up |
| [ipd-sample.json](ipd-sample.json) | IPD Sample JSON | Admission, two daily progress notes with vital signs and SOAP, admission medicine with times of day, discharge medicine with interval, In and Out diagnoses |
| [transfer-opd-to-ipd-sample.json](transfer-opd-to-ipd-sample.json) | Transfer OPD to IPD Sample JSON | One visit: the OPD consultation and the IPD admission as two encounters with their own codes |
| [referral-sample.json](referral-sample.json) | Referral Sample JSON | OPD visit referred out (discharge type Refer Out) with `referral.referred_to` |
| [form-anc-sample.json](form-anc-sample.json) | Form Sample JSON | OPD ANC visit with form ENTY-000H014 |

## Values to replace with NPCA values

- `FACILITY-CODE-FROM-NPCA`: the hospital's `health_facility_code`.
- `NPCA-SERVICE-CODE-TO-CONFIRM`: the NPCA service code of each billed
  service, entered by ADMIN at `/admin/npca` once NPCA confirms them.
- `SYNTHETIC-SPID-…` and `SYNTHETIC-CARD-…`: in a real visit these come from
  the beneficiary search.
- `RECEIVING-FACILITY-CODE-FROM-NPCA` and the `document_file` address in the
  referral: see questions 7 and 24 in [../OPEN-QUESTIONS.md](../OPEN-QUESTIONS.md).

## Making the pack again

```
node scripts/build-npca-approval-pack.mjs
```

It writes into this folder and exits with an error when a sample does not pass
the validation. The automated test `tests/v480-npca-pdf-alignment.test.mjs`
fails if these files are different from what the EMR builds today.
===== END FILE: docs/npca/approval-pack/README.md =====

===== BEGIN FILE: docs/npca/approval-pack/VALIDATION.md =====
# Validation of the sample JSON

Built by `node scripts/build-npca-approval-pack.mjs` from synthetic visits (no patient data).
Each file is the exact body the EMR would POST to the visit submission endpoint, checked by the EMR validation that runs before every real submission.

| File | Sample | Builder issues | Validation errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| opd-sample.json | OPD Sample JSON | 0 | 0 |
| ipd-sample.json | IPD Sample JSON | 0 | 0 |
| transfer-opd-to-ipd-sample.json | Transfer OPD to IPD Sample JSON | 0 | 0 |
| referral-sample.json | Referral Sample JSON | 0 | 0 |
| form-anc-sample.json | Form Sample JSON (OPD ANC, ENTY-000H014) | 0 | 0 |

Placeholders to replace with NPCA values: `FACILITY-CODE-FROM-NPCA` (health_facility_code), `NPCA-SERVICE-CODE-TO-CONFIRM` (service_code, entered by ADMIN at /admin/npca after NPCA confirms the service codes), the SPID / card numbers (returned by the beneficiary search) and the receiving facility code of the referral.
===== END FILE: docs/npca/approval-pack/VALIDATION.md =====

consent.procedure.text,"I, the undersigned, the patient or the legal representative of the patient named above, confirm that the doctor has explained to me, in a language I understand: the diagnosis, the proposed procedure, its expected benefits, its possible risks and complications, the other treatment options, and what may happen if the procedure is not done. I had the chance to ask questions and my questions were answered. I agree to the procedure named above, and to any additional procedure that becomes necessary during it to save life or prevent serious harm. ===== BEGIN FILE: public/npca.js =====
'use strict';
(async function(){
  const en=()=>window.getBorkeoLanguage?.()==='en', t=(kh,eng)=>en()?eng:kh;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id), esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  const statusNames={5:['កំពុងដំណើរការ','Processing'],3:['រង់ចាំការកែបន្ថែម','Pending'],2:['បរាជ័យ','Failed'],1:['រួចរាល់','Ready']};
  const statusName=n=>t(...(statusNames[n]||statusNames[3]));
  const failureReason=errors=>Array.isArray(errors)?errors.map(e=>typeof e==='string'?e:en()?e.messageEn||e.messageKh:e.messageKh||'').filter(Boolean).join('\n'):
    typeof errors==='string'?errors:errors?.remote||errors?.errors||'';
  let csrf='',canSubmit=false;
  async function api(path,body,method='POST'){const r=await fetch(path,{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',...(body?{method,headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','X-CSRF-Token':csrf},body:JSON.stringify(body)}:{})});const d=await r.json();if(!r.ok)throw Object.assign(Error(d.error||t('សូមពិនិត្យព័ត៌មានដែលត្រូវកែ។','Review the validation problems.')),{data:d});return d;}
  function showReview(data){
    $('npca-issues').replaceChildren();
    for(const error of data.errors||[]){const li=document.createElement('li'),a=document.createElement('a');a.textContent=en()?error.messageEn||error.messageKh:error.messageKh||t('សូមពិនិត្យព័ត៌មាន។','Review the details.');
      a.href=error.screen==='billing'?'/billing':error.screen==='anc'?'/anc-record?patient='+encodeURIComponent(data.patientKey):'/emr?patient='+encodeURIComponent(data.patientKey)+'&view='+({registration:'register',triage:'triage',daily:'daily',discharge:'discharge'}[error.screen]||'medical');li.appendChild(a);$('npca-issues').appendChild(li);}
    $('npca-detail').textContent=JSON.stringify(data.payload||data,null,2);if(!$('npca-review').open)$('npca-review').showModal();
  }
  async function load(){try{
    $('npca-refresh').textContent=t('ទាញទិន្នន័យ','Refresh');
    document.querySelector('h1').textContent=t('ការបញ្ជូនទិន្នន័យ NPCA','NPCA submissions');$('npca-payload-title').textContent=t('ទិន្នន័យសម្រាប់ពិនិត្យមុនបញ្ជូន','Payload for review');
    $('npca-head').innerHTML='<tr>'+[t('លេខករណី','Visit'),t('ស្ថានភាព','Status'),t('ព័ត៌មាន','Details'),t('សកម្មភាព','Actions')].map(s=>'<th>'+s+'</th>').join('')+'</tr>';
    const [data,candidates]=await Promise.all([api('/api/npca/submissions'),api('/api/npca/candidates')]);
    const rows=candidates.patients.map(p=>({...p,...data.submissions.find(s=>s.patient_key===p.patient_key)}));
    for(const s of data.submissions)if(!rows.some(r=>r.patient_key===s.patient_key))rows.push(s);
    $('npca-rows').innerHTML=rows.map(r=>`<tr><td><a href="/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(r.patient_key)}">${esc(r.case_number||r.visit_code)}</a></td><td>${esc(r.status?statusName(r.status):t('មិនទាន់បញ្ជូន','Not submitted'))}</td><td>${esc(r.message||'')}<pre>${esc(failureReason(r.errors))}</pre></td><td><button type="button" data-review="${esc(r.patient_key)}">${t('ពិនិត្យ','Review')}</button>${canSubmit&&![1,5].includes(r.status)?`<button type="button" data-submit="${esc(r.patient_key)}">${t('បញ្ជូន','Submit')}</button>`:''}</td></tr>`).join('');
    $('npca-status').textContent='';
  }catch(e){$('npca-status').textContent=e.message;}}
  try{const session=await api('/api/auth/me');csrf=session.csrfToken;canSubmit=['ADMIN','ACCOUNTANT','CLERK'].includes(session.user?.activeRole);
    if(location.pathname==='/admin/npca'){const s=await api('/api/npca/settings');$('npca-settings').hidden=false;$('npca-settings').textContent=[t('ការតភ្ជាប់៖ ','Connection: ')+(s.enabled?t('បើក','Enabled'):t('បិទ','Disabled')),t('អាសយដ្ឋាន៖ ','Address: ')+s.baseUrl,t('កូដមូលដ្ឋាន៖ ','Facility code: ')+s.facilityCode,t('ពេលទទួលសោចុងក្រោយ៖ ','Last token time: ')+(s.lastTokenAt?new Date(s.lastTokenAt).toLocaleString():t('មិនទាន់មាន','None'))].join('\n');$('npca-test').hidden=!s.canManage;$('npca-test').textContent=t('សាកល្បងការតភ្ជាប់','Test connection');
      if(s.canManage){$('npca-mappings').hidden=false;$('npca-map-title').textContent=t('ផ្គូផ្គងកូដដែល NPCA បានបញ្ជាក់','NPCA-confirmed code mappings');$('npca-map-save').textContent=t('រក្សាទុក','Save');}}
    $('npca-test').onclick=async()=>{try{await api('/api/npca/test',{});location.reload();}catch(e){$('npca-status').textContent=e.message;}};
    $('npca-map-save').onclick=async()=>{try{await api('/api/npca/mappings',{category:$('npca-map-category').value,localValue:$('npca-map-local').value.trim(),code:$('npca-map-code').value.trim()},'PUT');$('npca-status').textContent=t('បានរក្សាទុក','Saved');}catch(e){$('npca-status').textContent=e.message;}};
    $('npca-rows').onclick=async event=>{const button=event.target.closest('button');if(!button)return;const key=button.dataset.review||button.dataset.submit;if(!key)return;button.disabled=true;
      try{if(button.dataset.review)showReview({...await api('/api/npca/review?patientKey='+encodeURIComponent(key)),patientKey:key});else{await api('/api/npca/submit',{patientKey:key});await load();}}
      catch(error){if(error.data?.errors)showReview({...error.data,patientKey:key});else $('npca-status').textContent=error.message;}finally{button.disabled=false;}};
    // v48.0: one status check for every visit in a date range (NPCA guide 4.4).
    if(canSubmit){const today=new Date(Date.now()+7*3600000).toISOString().slice(0,10),from=new Date(Date.now()+7*3600000-30*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10);
      $('npca-range').hidden=false;$('npca-range-title').textContent=t('ពិនិត្យស្ថានភាពតាមកាលបរិច្ឆេទ','Check status by date');
      $('npca-range-by-label').textContent=t('តាម','By');$('npca-range-by').options[0].textContent=t('ថ្ងៃចេញ','Discharge date');$('npca-range-by').options[1].textContent=t('ថ្ងៃចូល','Admission date');
      $('npca-range-from-label').textContent=t('ពីថ្ងៃ','From');$('npca-range-to-label').textContent=t('ដល់ថ្ងៃ','To');$('npca-range-check').textContent=t('ពិនិត្យស្ថានភាព','Check status');
      $('npca-range-from').value=from;$('npca-range-to').value=today;
      $('npca-range-check').onclick=async()=>{const button=$('npca-range-check');button.disabled=true;
        try{const r=await api('/api/npca/check-processed',{from:$('npca-range-from').value,to:$('npca-range-to').value,by:$('npca-range-by').value});await load();
          $('npca-status').textContent=t('ចំនួនករណីដែលបានពិនិត្យ៖ ','Visits checked: ')+r.checked+'  '+t('ចំនួនដែលបានធ្វើបច្ចុប្បន្នភាព៖ ','Updated: ')+r.updated;}
        catch(e){$('npca-status').textContent=e.message;}finally{button.disabled=false;}};}
    $('npca-refresh').onclick=load;await load();
  }catch(e){$('npca-status').textContent=e.message;}
})();

===== BEGIN FILE: scripts/build-npca-approval-pack.mjs =====
// v48.0 - NPCA approval pack: the sample JSON listed in "Related documents" of
// the NPCA EMR Clinical Data Integration Specification v1.4 (OPD, IPD,
// transfer OPD to IPD, referral, clinical form), built by the EMR's own payload
// builder from SYNTHETIC visits and checked by the EMR's own validation with the
// real ICD-10 list, CMS medicine codes and NCDD village list. Usage:
//   node scripts/build-npca-approval-pack.mjs [output folder]
// (default docs/npca/approval-pack). No database file, no patient data, no network.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { openDatabase } from '../server/database.mjs';
import { ensureNpcaSchema } from '../server/npca/storage.mjs';
import { setCodeOverride } from '../server/npca/codes.mjs';
import { buildVisitPayload } from '../server/npca/visit-builder.mjs';
import { validateVisitPayload } from '../server/npca/validation.mjs';
import { canonicalAddress, validAddress } from '../server/npca/address.mjs';
import { createDiagnosisSearch } from '../server/icd10.mjs';
import { medicineCatalogue } from '../server/medicine-catalogue.mjs';

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
// Placeholders NPCA supplies; the service code is entered by ADMIN at /admin/npca.
export const FACILITY_PLACEHOLDER = 'FACILITY-CODE-FROM-NPCA';
const SERVICE_PLACEHOLDER = 'NPCA-SERVICE-CODE-TO-CONFIRM';
const at = (day, time) => `2026-10-${day}T${time}`;
const vitals = (temp, pulse, resp, sys, dia, spo2) => [['Temperature', temp], ['Heart Rate', pulse], ['Respiratory Rate', resp], ['Systolic Blood Pressure', sys], ['Diastolic Blood Pressure', dia], ['Blood Oxygen', spo2]].map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value }));
const medicine = (code) => { const item = medicineCatalogue().items.find((m) => m.code === code); return { cmsCode: code, medicine_name: item.name, strength: item.strength, form: item.form }; };

function patient(key, { surname, name, sex, birthdate, phone }) {
  return { patientKey: 'case:SYNTHETIC-' + key, healthId: 'SYNTHETIC-' + key, surname, name, sex, birthdate, phone, nationality: 'KHM', maritalStatus: 'Married',
    occupation: 'Farmer', disabilities: [],
    address: canonicalAddress({ province: { code: '16' }, district: { code: '1603' }, commune: { code: '160301' }, village: { code: '16030104' } }),
    identifications: [{ card_code: 'SYNTHETIC-CARD-' + key, card_type: 'HEF' }] };
}
function base(key, person, mode, { day = '20', start = '08:00', end = '10:00' } = {}) {
  const encounterId = 'initial-' + mode;
  return { patient: patient(key, person), link: { spid: 'SYNTHETIC-SPID-' + key, eligible: 1, payment_type: 'PATY-0000N09' },
    createdAt: at(day, start), updatedAt: at(day, end), visitType: mode, admissionType: 'Walk-in', admittedAt: at(day, start), completed: true,
    dischargeType: 'Authorized', outcome: 'Improved', outpatients: [], inpatients: [], emergencies: [], surgeries: [], progress_notes: [], dailyVitals: [],
    medical_histories: [{ encounterId, name: 'History of Present Illness', value: ['Synthetic history: symptoms for 3 days.'] }, { encounterId, name: 'Allergies', value: ['No known drug allergy'] }],
    physical_examinations: [{ encounterId, name: 'General Appearance', value: ['Alert, mildly ill'], value_type: 'array', value_unit: null }],
    laboratories: [], imageries: [], diagnoses: [], soaps: [], prescriptions: [], consultations: ['Drink more water', 'Return if fever persists'], invoices: [], forms: [], sourceIssues: [],
    triages: [{ id: 'initial', chief_complaint: 'Synthetic complaint', height: 160, weight: 55, recorded_at: at(day, start.replace(/:00$/, ':10')), recorded_by: 'Synthetic triage nurse', title: 'Nurse', observations: vitals(38.2, 96, 20, 118, 76, 97) }],
    encounterId };
}
const diagnosis = (encounterId, type, code, name, when, who = 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor') => ({ encounterId, diagnosis_type_name: type, diagnosis_code: code, diagnosis_name: name, diagnosed_at: when, diagnosed_by: who, title: 'Doctor' });
const invoice = (id, when, total, medicines = []) => ({ id, paymentType: 'HEF_POOR', date: when, total, cashier: 'Synthetic cashier',
  services: [{ localCode: 'SYNTHETIC_SERVICE', service_name: 'Consultation', service_category: 'Consultation', quantity: 1, price: total, paid: false }], medications: medicines });
const lab = (encounterId, when) => ({ released: true, encounterId, request_code: 'SYNTHETIC-LAB-1', requested_at: when, requested_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor',
  results: [{ name: 'Hemoglobin', category: 'Hematology', value: 11.2, value_type: 'float', value_unit: 'g/dL', reference_range: '12.0-16.0', interpretation: 'Low',
    verified_at: when.replace(/T(\d\d)/, (m, h) => 'T' + String(Number(h) + 1).padStart(2, '0')), verified_by: 'Synthetic lab doctor', recorded_at: when.replace(/T(\d\d)/, (m, h) => 'T' + String(Number(h) + 1).padStart(2, '0')), recorded_by: 'Synthetic lab technician' }] });

export function sampleSources() {
  // 1. OPD: consultation, lab, home prescription, invoice.
  const opd = base('OPD', { surname: 'Synthetic', name: 'Opd', sex: 'F', birthdate: '1990-05-15', phone: '012345678' }, 'opd');
  opd.dischargedAt = at('20', '10:00');
  opd.outpatients.push({ id: opd.encounterId, is_new: true, name: 'OPD General Medicine', service_type: 'General Consultation', started_at: at('20', '08:30'), ended_at: at('20', '10:00'), encountered_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor', symptoms: ['Fever', 'Diarrhoea'] });
  opd.diagnoses.push(diagnosis(opd.encounterId, 'In', 'A09', 'Infectious gastroenteritis and colitis, unspecified', at('20', '08:40')), diagnosis(opd.encounterId, 'Out', 'A09', 'Infectious gastroenteritis and colitis, unspecified', at('20', '09:50')));
  opd.soaps.push({ id: 'initial', encounterId: opd.encounterId, subjective: 'Fever and loose stools for 3 days.', objective: 'T 38.2, mild dehydration.', assessment: 'Acute gastroenteritis.', plan: 'Oral rehydration, paracetamol, review in 3 days.', recorded_at: at('20', '08:45'), recorded_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor' });
  opd.laboratories.push(lab(opd.encounterId, at('20', '08:50')));
  opd.prescriptions.push({ id: 'opd', home: true, encounterId: null, prescribed_at: at('20', '09:50'), prescribed_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor',
    medications: [{ ...medicine('AA0060'), method: 'Oral', unit: 'Tablet', morning: 2, afternoon: 0, evening: 2, night: 0, days: 5, dispense: { quantity: 20, unit: 'Tablet' } }] });
  opd.invoices.push(invoice('opd', at('20', '10:00'), 15000));
  opd.followupAt = at('23', '08:00');

  // 2. IPD: admission, two daily progress notes with vital signs and SOAP, discharge medicines.
  const ipd = base('IPD', { surname: 'Synthetic', name: 'Ipd', sex: 'M', birthdate: '1975-02-01', phone: '097123456' }, 'ipd', { day: '18', start: '09:00' });
  ipd.dischargedAt = at('21', '10:00');
  ipd.inpatients.push({ id: ipd.encounterId, is_new: true, name: 'Internal Medicine Ward', service_type: 'Internal Medicine', started_at: at('18', '09:30'), ended_at: at('21', '10:00'), encountered_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor', symptoms: ['Cough', 'Fever'] });

  for (const [index, day] of ['19', '20'].entries()) {
    const id = 'daily-' + day;
    ipd.progress_notes.push({ id, encounterId: ipd.encounterId, started_at: at(day, '08:00'), ended_at: at(day, '08:20'), encountered_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor' });
    ipd.dailyVitals.push({ encounterId: id, recorded_at: at(day, '08:00'), recorded_by: 'Synthetic ward nurse', title: 'Nurse', observations: vitals(index ? 37 : 37.8, index ? 84 : 92, 18, 120, 78, 97) });
    ipd.soaps.push({ id, encounterId: id, subjective: 'Less cough.', objective: 'Crackles right base.', assessment: 'Pneumonia improving.', plan: 'Continue antibiotic.', evaluation: 'Improving', recorded_at: at(day, '08:10'), recorded_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor' });
  }
  ipd.prescriptions.push({ id: 'admission', home: false, encounterId: ipd.encounterId, prescribed_at: at('18', '09:45'), prescribed_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor',
    medications: [{ ...medicine('AA0060'), method: 'Oral', unit: 'Tablet', morning: 2, afternoon: 2, evening: 2, night: 0, days: 3, dispense: { quantity: 18, unit: 'Tablet' } }] },
  { id: 'home', home: true, encounterId: null, prescribed_at: at('21', '09:55'), prescribed_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor',
    medications: [{ ...medicine('AA0060'), method: 'Oral', unit: 'Tablet', interval: '8h', days: 4, note: 'Complete the course.', dispense: { quantity: 24, unit: 'Tablet' } }] });
  ipd.invoices.push(invoice('ipd', at('21', '10:00'), 120000));

  // 3. Transfer OPD to IPD: the OPD consultation stays as the first encounter of the same visit.
  const transfer = base('TRANSFER', { surname: 'Synthetic', name: 'Transfer', sex: 'F', birthdate: '1988-07-07', phone: '011223344' }, 'ipd', { day: '19', start: '08:00' });

  transfer.inpatients.push({ id: transfer.encounterId, is_new: true, name: 'Internal Medicine Ward', service_type: 'Internal Medicine', started_at: at('19', '09:40'), ended_at: at('21', '15:00'), encountered_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor' });
  transfer.soaps.push({ id: 'opd', encounterId: 'before-admission', subjective: 'High fever.', objective: 'T 39.4.', assessment: 'Malaria suspected, admit.', plan: 'Admit to ward.', recorded_at: at('19', '09:00'), recorded_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor' });
  transfer.diagnoses.push(diagnosis(transfer.encounterId, 'In', 'B50', 'Plasmodium falciparum malaria', at('19', '09:40')), diagnosis(transfer.encounterId, 'Out', 'B50', 'Plasmodium falciparum malaria', at('21', '14:50')));
  transfer.progress_notes.push({ id: 'daily-20', encounterId: transfer.encounterId, started_at: at('20', '08:00'), ended_at: at('20', '08:15'), encountered_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor' });
  transfer.dailyVitals.push({ encounterId: 'daily-20', recorded_at: at('20', '08:00'), recorded_by: 'Synthetic ward nurse', title: 'Nurse', observations: vitals(37.4, 88, 18, 116, 74, 98) });
  transfer.soaps.push({ id: 'daily-20', encounterId: 'daily-20', subjective: 'No fever since night.', objective: 'Afebrile.', assessment: 'Responding.', plan: 'Continue treatment.', recorded_at: at('20', '08:10'), recorded_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor' });

  const referral = base('REFERRAL', { surname: 'Synthetic', name: 'Referral', sex: 'M', birthdate: '1960-03-03', phone: '016778899' }, 'opd', { day: '20', start: '13:00', end: '14:30' });
  referral.dischargedAt = at('20', '14:30'); referral.dischargeType = 'Refer Out'; referral.outcome = 'Unchanged';
  referral.outpatients.push({ id: referral.encounterId, is_new: true, name: 'OPD Surgery', service_type: 'General Consultation', started_at: at('20', '13:20'), ended_at: at('20', '14:30'), encountered_by: 'Dr. Synthetic Doctor', title: 'Doctor', symptoms: ['Right lower abdominal pain'] });

  referral.invoices.push(invoice('referral', at('20', '14:30'), 15000));
  referral.referral = { referred_to: { referral_number: 'SYNTHETIC/REF/2026/001', transportation: 'Ambulance', reason: 'Acute appendicitis needing surgery', has_called: true,

    medications: 'IV fluid started 14:00', document_file: 'https://hospital.example/referrals/SYNTHETIC-REF-2026-001.pdf', health_facility: { name: 'Synthetic Referral Hospital', code: 'RECEIVING-FACILITY-CODE-FROM-NPCA', label: 'Referral hospital', type: 'CPA3' } } };

  // 5. Clinical data form: OPD ANC visit with form ENTY-000H014.
  const anc = base('ANC', { surname: 'Synthetic', name: 'Anc', sex: 'F', birthdate: '1998-09-09', phone: '010556677' }, 'opd', { day: '22', start: '08:00', end: '09:00' });
  anc.dischargedAt = at('22', '09:00'); anc.consultations = ['Iron and folic acid daily', 'Danger signs explained'];
  anc.outpatients.push({ id: anc.encounterId, is_new: false, name: 'ANC', service_type: 'Antenatal care', started_at: at('22', '08:20'), ended_at: at('22', '09:00'), encountered_by: 'Synthetic Midwife', title: 'Midwife' });
  anc.diagnoses.push(diagnosis(anc.encounterId, 'In', 'Z34.9', 'Encounter for supervision of normal pregnancy, unspecified', at('22', '08:30'), 'Synthetic Midwife'), diagnosis(anc.encounterId, 'Out', 'Z34.9', 'Encounter for supervision of normal pregnancy, unspecified', at('22', '08:55'), 'Synthetic Midwife'));
  anc.soaps.push({ id: 'initial', encounterId: anc.encounterId, subjective: 'No complaint.', objective: 'Fundal height 26 cm, FHR 140.', assessment: 'Normal pregnancy 26 weeks.', plan: 'Next ANC in 4 weeks.', recorded_at: at('22', '08:40'), recorded_by: 'Synthetic Midwife', title: 'Midwife' });
